- Evidence: Fetch is parameterless and filtering is local — AdminInvoicesPage.tsx:125 `const res = await adminApi.invoices();` and :154-160 `const filtered = invoices.filter((inv) => { const matchSearch = !search || inv.invoice_number.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || inv.business?.name?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()); const matchStatus = statusFilter === 'all' || inv.status === statusFilter; return matchSearch && matchStatus; });`, with the Select offering all five statuses at :315-321. The server already implements exactly these filters and they go unused — admin.service.ts:538-546 `if (query.status) where.status = query.status; if (query.businessId) where.business_id = que
- Fix: Pass `{ status: statusFilter === 'all' ? undefined : statusFilter, search, page, limit }` to adminApi.invoices, refetch on filter change (debounce the search), and take the tile totals from `meta.total` plus a dedicated aggregate rather than the current page.

**Admin logout never revokes the refresh token server-side**
- `frontend/src/components/layout/AdminLayout.tsx:40` — *auth-enforcement*
- Impact: Clicking Logout in the admin panel only clears local redux/localStorage. The 7-day rotating refresh token row stays in the database, so anyone who has captured it (shared machine, copied storage, backup) can keep minting fresh super-admin access tokens indefinitely. The auth service has a logout endpoint that deletes those rows; it is simply not called.
- Evidence: AdminLayout.tsx:40-44 `const handleLogout = () => { dispatch(logout()); dispatch(clearBusiness()); navigate('/login'); };` — no API call (the file's only imports from the store are `logout` from '@/store/authSlice' and `clearBusiness`, AdminLayout.tsx:16-17). Wired to the menu item at :217-220. The server-side revocation exists — packages/auth-service/src/services/auth.service.ts:489-501 `async logout(userId, refreshToken?) { if (refreshToken) { await prisma.refreshToken.deleteMany({ where: { user_id: userId, token: refreshToken } }); } else { await prisma.refreshToken.deleteMany({ where: { user_id: userId } }); } }`, and refresh keeps working off that row (auth.service.ts:461-478).
- Fix: Await `authApi.logout({ refreshToken })` (adding the method to frontend/src/lib/api.ts if absent) before dispatching the local logout, in a try/finally so the client still clears state if the call fails. Same fix applies to AppLayout.tsx:65.

**AdminPage.tsx is dead code that would silently render invented users and audit logs if ever routed**
- `frontend/src/components/admin/AdminPage.tsx:10` — *dead-code*
- Impact: Confirmed unreachable: nothing imports it, so it is pure dead weight in the bundle-adjacent source tree. It matters because it is a trap — it duplicates the admin dashboard with the same broken `stats.revenue` field and a catch block that fabricates users and audit logs, so anyone who wires it up (the name makes it the obvious candidate for a /admin route) inherits both bugs.
- Evidence: `grep -rn "AdminPage" frontend/src` matches only /Users/yashjain/Documents/Vercel Code/bahiKhataMainApp/frontend/src/components/admin/AdminPage.tsx itself — no import anywhere. frontend/src/App.tsx:48-56 lazily imports AdminDashboardPage/AdminUsersPage/AdminAuditPage/AdminSettingsPage/AdminBusinessesPage/AdminPlansPage/AdminSubscriptionsPage/AdminInvoicesPage and App.tsx:268-277 routes /admin, /admin/users, /admin/audit, /admin/settings, /admin/businesses, /admin/plans, /admin/subscriptions, /admin/invoices and /admin/* — AdminPage is absent from both lists. The latent bugs: AdminPage.tsx:74 `<StatCard title="MRR" value={\`₹${(stats.revenue / 1000).toFixed(0)}k\`} ...>` against the server's 
- Fix: Delete frontend/src/components/admin/AdminPage.tsx (per the repo's no-git-repo rule, move it to .trash-cleanup/ rather than removing it outright).

**Feature-flag endpoints exist but no frontend code can reach them; AdminSettingsPage repurposes the same table for platform settings**
- `packages/admin-service/src/routes/admin.routes.ts:45` — *endpoint-unused*
- Impact: Per-business feature flags cannot be created, listed or toggled from the admin console at all — there is no UI and no client method. Meanwhile Platform Settings writes PLATFORM_* rows into the same feature_flags table, so any future flag admin screen will show a dozen pseudo-flags mixed in with real ones (getFeatureFlags does not filter them out).
- Evidence: Three routes are registered — admin.routes.ts:44-47 `router.get('/feature-flags', ctrl.getFeatureFlags); router.post('/feature-flags', ctrl.createFeatureFlag); router.patch('/feature-flags/:flagId', ctrl.toggleFeatureFlag);` backed by admin.service.ts:619-643. No caller exists: adminApi (frontend/src/lib/api.ts:474-507) has no feature-flag method, and `grep -rn "feature-flags\|featureFlag" frontend/src` returns nothing. AdminSettingsPage's only API calls are api.ts:505-506 `getSettings: () => api.get('/admin/settings')` and `updateSettings: (data) => api.put('/admin/settings', data)`, and the page renders a fixed hardcoded set of 12 controls (AdminSettingsPage.tsx:14-42) rather than anything
- Fix: Either add `featureFlags/createFeatureFlag/toggleFeatureFlag` to adminApi plus a flags section in AdminSettingsPage, or remove the three unused routes. Regardless, exclude `PLATFORM_*` from getFeatureFlags (`where: { feature: { not: { startsWith: 'PLATFORM_' } }, ... }`) and consider a separate table for platform settings.

### Auth & session

**Dev-mock request abort is keyed on import.meta.env.DEV alone, so a stale dev token wedges the app once VITE_DEV_SKIP_LOGIN is off**
- `frontend/src/lib/api.ts:117` — *broken-handler*
- Impact: A developer uses "Skip Login", then removes VITE_DEV_SKIP_LOGIN and restarts. localStorage still holds the dev-mock token, so isAuthenticated is true but App.tsx skips the mock branch and issues real calls, which the request interceptor silently aborts. Every page renders empty with no error and no way to reach /login except the Logout button; the dev-login button is gone because isDevMode() is false.
- Evidence: The abort guard ignores the VITE_DEV_SKIP_LOGIN flag — frontend/src/lib/api.ts:117-122: if (token?.startsWith('dev-mock-') && import.meta.env.DEV) { const controller = new AbortController(); config.signal = controller.signal; controller.abort('Dev mode: skipping real API call'); return config; } whereas the mock-data branch in App.tsx additionally requires the flag — frontend/src/App.tsx:103-107: if (isDevMode() && token.startsWith('dev-mock-')) { dispatch(setUser(DEV_USER)); dispatch(setBusinesses(DEV_BUSINESSES)); return; } with frontend/src/lib/mock-data.ts:51-53: export function isDevMode(): boolean { return import.meta.env.DEV && import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_SKIP_LOGIN === 'true'; } The can
- Fix: Use isDevMode() in the request interceptor guard, and when a dev-mock token is present while isDevMode() is false, dispatch logout() so the app returns to /login.

### Business management

**"not found" cases in bank-account and credit-card CRUD return HTTP 500 instead of 404**
- `packages/business-service/src/services/business.service.ts:457` — *error-handling*
- Impact: Acting on a row another session already deleted (Edit/Delete on a stale bank-account or card row) produces a 500 whose body in production is the generic "Internal server error", so the UI toast is "Failed to delete" with no indication the record is simply gone, and the 500 pollutes error monitoring as a server fault.
- Evidence: Plain Errors are thrown instead of the AppError subclasses that exist for exactly this: business.service.ts:457 `if (!existing) throw new Error('Bank account not found');`, :484 same in deleteBankAccount, :617 `if (!card) throw new Error('Credit card not found');`, :663 and :686 likewise; and business.service.ts:155 `throw new Error('Primary business cannot be deactivated. You can only edit it.');`. The handler only maps AppError to a status — errors.ts:66 `if (err instanceof AppError) { ... res.status(err.statusCode).json(...) }` — everything else falls through to errors.ts:89-92 `res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'Internal server error'
- Fix: Throw `new NotFoundError('Bank account')` / `new NotFoundError('Credit card')` and `new BadRequestError(...)` for the primary-business guard so callers get 404/400 with a usable message.

### Dashboard & Reports

**Reports date-range picker has no effect on the Outstanding tab but still triggers a refetch**
- `frontend/src/components/reports/ReportsPage.tsx:171` — *dead-ui*
- Impact: On Reports -> Outstanding, changing the From/To dates or pressing Refresh re-issues the request and re-renders, but the numbers never change — the endpoint is called with no date arguments and the backend ignores dates entirely. The user believes they are looking at outstanding balances 'for the period' shown in the Period card.
- Evidence: frontend/src/components/reports/ReportsPage.tsx:105-107 — `useEffect(() => { fetchReport(tab); }, [tab, dateRange]);` (refires on every date change) frontend/src/components/reports/ReportsPage.tsx:171 — `const res = await ledgerApi.outstanding();` — called with no params, while frontend/src/lib/api.ts:395 supports them: `outstanding: (params?: any) => api.get('/ledger/outstanding', { params }),` The controller only ever reads `type` — packages/ledger-service/src/controllers/ledger.controller.ts:50-55: `const type = req.query.type as 'receivable' | 'payable' | undefined; const result = await ledgerService.getOutstandingSummary(businessId, type);` and the service has no date parameter at all —
- Fix: Either hide the Period card when `tab === 'outstanding'` (and label the tab 'as of now'), or push the Outstanding filter server-side: pass `type` from the existing Select (`outstandingFilter`) so the backend does the filtering, and add an as-on-date to getOutstandingSummary computed from ledger entries rather than the live Party.balance.

### Expenses & Bills

**Export CSV/Excel leaves the Type column blank — nested key "expense_type.name" is looked up flat**
- `frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:34` — *data-bug*
- Impact: Export > CSV (or Excel) from the Expenses page downloads a file whose expense_type.name column is empty on every row, so the exported spreadsheet has amounts and dates but no expense type. The toast still says the export succeeded.
- Evidence: frontend/src/components/expenses/ExpensesPage.tsx:254-258 `<ExportButton data={expenses} columns={['expense_date', 'expense_type.name', 'amount', 'source', 'is_paid']} filename="expenses" />` frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:32-38 `const rows = data.map(row => headers.map(h => { const val = row[h]; const str = val == null ? '' : ... }).join(','))` — `row['expense_type.name']` is undefined because the API returns a nested object (`include: { expense_type: true }`, packages/expense-service/src/services/expense.service.ts:101).
- Fix: Resolve dotted paths in dataToCSV (`h.split('.').reduce((o, k) => o?.[k], row)`), which also fixes every other page passing nested column keys to ExportButton.

**Export > PDF only shows a "coming soon" success toast**
- `frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:76` — *dead-ui*
- Impact: On the Expenses page the Export dropdown offers "Export PDF"; clicking it pops a green success toast and downloads nothing. The affordance is indistinguishable from the working CSV/JSON items.
- Evidence: frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:71-77 `} else if (format === 'json') { ... } else { toast.success(t('pdf_coming_soon')); }` reached from :116-119 `<DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => handleExport('pdf')}> ... {t('export_pdf')}`; ExpensesPage passes no onExport override (ExpensesPage.tsx:254-258 supplies only data/columns/filename).
- Fix: Hide the PDF item when no onExport handler is supplied, or implement client-side PDF generation.

**Orphaned second axios client for expenses (wrong baseURL, wrong token key, no x-business-id) shadowed by lib/api.ts**
- `frontend/src/lib/api/expenses.ts:1` — *missing-wiring*
- Impact: Currently dead code — nothing imports it, so no user-visible breakage today. It is a live trap: `@/lib/api` resolves to the file frontend/src/lib/api.ts (a file beats a directory in Vite/bundler resolution), so all 47 importers get the correct client. The moment someone deletes api.ts, renames it, or imports '@/lib/api/expenses' directly, every expense call would go to /api/expenses/... (missing the /v1 segment), carry a token read from the never-written localStorage key 'auth_token', omit the x-business-id header entirely (=> 400 "Missing x-business-id header"), and its 401 interceptor would hard-redirect to /login, logging the user out.
- Evidence: `grep -rn "api/expenses" frontend/src` returns zero matches — no importer. frontend/src/lib/api/index.ts:5 `baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '/api',` vs frontend/src/lib/api.ts:5 `const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '/api/v1';` frontend/src/lib/api/index.ts:15 `const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token');` vs the real keys written by frontend/src/store/authSlice.ts:47-48 `localStorage.setItem('bk_token', ...); localStorage.setItem('bk_refresh_token', ...)`; lib/api/index.ts also never sets x-business-id, which frontend/src/lib/api.ts:127-129 does (`config.headers['x-business-id'] = businessId;`) and which every expense controller requires (packages/expense-service/s
- Fix: Delete frontend/src/lib/api/expenses.ts and frontend/src/lib/api/index.ts; expenseApi already lives in frontend/src/lib/api.ts:510-524.

**Expense stat cards mix time windows and count future-dated expenses as "today"**
- `packages/expense-service/src/services/expense.service.ts:286` — *data-bug*
- Impact: The four cards on the Expenses page are not comparable: "Today" and "This Month" count only standalone expenses (excluding every purchase/sale-linked expense recorded in the same period), while "Purchase Expenses" and "Sale Expenses" are lifetime totals. Also, an expense dated in the future is counted in "Today" because the filter is open-ended, so a post-dated entry inflates today's number permanently.
- Evidence: packages/expense-service/src/services/expense.service.ts:285-289 `prisma.expense.aggregate({ where: { business_id: businessId, expense_date: { gte: today } }, _sum: { amount: true }, _count: true })` — `gte` with no `lt` upper bound for the "today" bucket. :295-302 `prisma.purchaseExpense.aggregate({ where: { purchase: { business_id: businessId, status: { not: 'CANCELLED' } } }, _sum: { amount: true } })` and the saleExpense equivalent — no date bound at all. :305-319 `return { today: {...}, thisMonth: {...}, overall: { standalone: Number(standaloneMonth._sum.amount || 0), purchase: Number(purchaseExpensesTotal._sum.amount || 0), sale: Number(saleExpensesTotal._sum.amount || 0), total: ... }
- Fix: Bound the today bucket with `lt: tomorrow`, and either include purchase/sale expenses in the today/thisMonth sums or scope the purchase/sale aggregates to the same month so the four cards describe one period.

### Inventory

**Notes field on the stock adjust form is silently discarded**
- `frontend/src/components/inventory/StockAdjustPage.tsx:198` — *payload-mismatch*
- Impact: The "Notes (optional)" textarea on /inventory/adjust accepts free text that is never stored — only the dropdown `reason` is persisted as the transaction note, so the user's explanation of the adjustment is lost with no indication.
- Evidence: StockAdjustPage.tsx:196-204 binds `form.notes` (`onChange={(e) => setForm(p => ({ ...p, notes: e.target.value }))}`) and the whole form is posted at line 64. The service signature accepts only `{ itemId; quantity; reason; type }` (inventory.service.ts:192-194) and writes `notes: data.reason` when creating the transaction (inventory.service.ts:237), never touching `data.notes`.
- Fix: Persist both, e.g. `notes: [data.reason, data.notes].filter(Boolean).join(' — ')`, or remove the textarea.

**listItems lowStock=true branch puts an unawaited Promise into the Prisma where clause (guaranteed 500)**
- `packages/inventory-service/src/services/inventory.service.ts:93` — *broken-handler*
- Impact: Any caller of GET /inventory/items?lowStock=true gets a 500. No current inventory page sends that param (the tab filters client-side), so it is latent today, but it is a live trap for anyone wiring the low-stock tab to the server, and on the frontend a 500 here silently swaps in the hardcoded demo items.
- Evidence: inventory.service.ts:92-94: `if (query.lowStock === 'true') { where.current_stock = { lte: prisma.$queryRaw`"min_stock"` }; }` — `$queryRaw` is invoked as a tag function and returns a Promise, which is then handed to Prisma as a comparison value; Prisma will reject it with a validation error. Column-to-column comparison is not expressible this way in the query builder (contrast the working raw SQL at inventory.service.ts:141-148).
- Fix: Delete the branch and route low-stock requests to the existing `getLowStockItems` raw query, or use Prisma's `$queryRaw` for the whole filtered list.

### Ledger

**Send reminder reports success even when the request fails**
- `frontend/src/components/ledger/PartyLedgerPage.tsx:57` — *broken-handler*
- Impact: Party Ledger -> "Send reminder": if the notification service is down, the party has no phone/WhatsApp number, or the call 4xx/5xxs, the user still gets a green toast and assumes the reminder went out. No reminder is ever sent and nothing surfaces the failure.
- Evidence: PartyLedgerPage.tsx:57-64 `const handleSendReminder = async () => { try { await notificationApi.sendReminder(partyId!); toast.success(t('ledger:reminder_sent')); } catch { toast.success(t('ledger:reminder_queued')); } };` — the catch branch is also `toast.success`, and nothing is actually queued client-side. The endpoint itself is wired correctly (api.ts:452 `sendReminder: (partyId) => api.post('/notifications/payment-reminder', { partyId })` -> packages/notification-service/src/routes/notification.routes.ts:21 `router.post('/payment-reminder', ctrl.sendPaymentReminder);` -> controllers/notification.controller.ts:31 `const { partyId } = req.body;`), so this is purely the client masking real 
- Fix: Use `toast.error(err.response?.data?.message || t('ledger:reminder_failed'))` in the catch.

### Parties & Cutters

**Cutter phone/party name validation mismatch — backend 400s are shown as a generic failure toast**
- `frontend/src/components/shared/PartiesPage.tsx:95` — *payload-mismatch*
- Impact: In Add/Edit Cutter, typing a phone in any form other than exactly 10 digits starting 6-9 (e.g. "98765 43210", a landline, or +91 prefix) makes Save fail with only "Failed to add cutter" and no field highlight — the user has no idea which field is wrong. Same class of dead-end on Add Party: a 1-character name, or a malformed IFSC in the Banking section, returns "Validation failed" with no field information while the per-field `errors` array from the API is discarded.
- Evidence: Cutter phone input has no filtering/validation — frontend/src/components/shared/PartiesPage.tsx:95 `<Input placeholder={t('parties:phone_placeholder_short')} value={phone} onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} />` and the payload at :57-62 sends it raw; the backend is strict: packages/profile-service/src/shared/validators/index.ts:148-153 `export const createCutterSchema = z.object({ name: z.string().min(2).max(100), phone: phoneSchema.optional(), ... })` with :8 `export const phoneSchema = z.string().regex(/^[6-9]\d{9}$/, 'Invalid Indian phone number');`. The error is swallowed: PartiesPage.tsx:71-73 `} catch { toast.error(isEdit ? t('parties:cutter_update_fail') : t('parties:cutter_add_
- Fix: Mirror the party dialog's digit filter + 10-digit check on the cutter phone field, enforce name min-length 2 and the IFSC regex client-side, and render `err.response?.data?.errors` field-by-field instead of a single generic toast.

**Pincode can only be picked from the India Post API; no manual entry when the lookup returns nothing**
- `frontend/src/components/shared/AddPartyDialog.tsx:801` — *dead-ui*
- Impact: In the Address section, after choosing a state and city, the Pincode control is a read-only dropdown fed exclusively by an external API. For cities the India Post endpoint does not match (or whenever that request fails/is blocked/offline) the dropdown shows "No results" and the user simply cannot record a pincode for the party. The City dropdown has the same shape: it is limited to a hard-coded list of ~8-24 towns per state, so parties in any smaller town cannot have city recorded at all.
- Evidence: frontend/src/components/shared/AddPartyDialog.tsx:798-810 `<SearchableDropdown placeholder={addr.city ? t('parties:select_pincode') : t('parties:select_city_first')} value={addr.pincode} options={pincodeOptions} onChange={v => updateAddr('pincode', v.split('—')[0].trim())} disabled={!addr.city} loading={pincodesLoading} />` (identical in EditPartyDialog.tsx:844-856). SearchableDropdown only emits values from `options` — AddPartyDialog.tsx:109 `const select = (val: string) => { onChange(val); setOpen(false); setSearch(''); };` with no free-text commit path. Options come solely from the remote call: frontend/src/lib/india-geo.ts:409-419 `export async function fetchPincodesForCity(city: string)
- Fix: Allow free-text commit in SearchableDropdown (accept the typed search value on Enter when it matches /^[1-9][0-9]{5}$/ for pincode) or render a plain Input with the API results as suggestions, and do the same for city.

### Profile, Notifications, Help

**Notification type vocabulary mismatch: the frontend's icon/colour map matches no type the backend can produce**
- `frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationsPage.tsx:13` — *response-shape*
- Impact: Payment reminders and bill reminders — the only notifications the product actually generates besides low stock — all render with the generic cyan Info icon, so users cannot distinguish a money reminder from a system message at a glance, and the type_* labels shipped in all 11 locales are never displayed.
- Evidence: NotificationsPage.tsx:13-29 keys everything on `PURCHASE | SALE | PAYMENT | LOW_STOCK | ALERT | INFO` (`const iconMap: Record<string, React.ElementType> = { PURCHASE: ShoppingCart, SALE: TrendingUp, PAYMENT: CreditCard, LOW_STOCK: Package, ALERT: AlertTriangle, INFO: Info };`) with the fallback at :122-123 `const Icon = iconMap[notification.type] || Info;`. The only writer of notifications in the whole repo is notification-service (grep for `notification.create` matches only notification.service.ts:21 and :50) and it emits exactly three literals: `type: 'PAYMENT_REMINDER'` (:182), `type: 'BILL_REMINDER'` (:301) and `type: 'LOW_STOCK'` (:343) — the schema.prisma:1135-1144 enum (PAYMENT_REMIND
- Fix: Re-key iconMap/colorMap on the Prisma enum values (PAYMENT_REMINDER, BILL_REMINDER, LOW_STOCK, SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRY, NEW_PURCHASE, NEW_SALE, SYSTEM, WHATSAPP), rename the locale type_* keys to match, and render the label next to the title.

**Help FAQ content and category filters are hardcoded English although translations exist in all 11 locales**
- `frontend/src/components/help/HelpSupportPage.tsx:20` — *i18n*
- Impact: A Hindi/Tamil/Bengali user switching language sees the page chrome translated but all 10 questions, all 10 answers and the 4 category buttons stay in English — and the help search box only matches English words, so searching in the chosen language returns "No FAQs found".
- Evidence: HelpSupportPage.tsx:20-71 hardcodes the content (`const faqs: FAQ[] = [{ category: 'Getting Started', question: 'How do I create a new business?', answer: 'Go to the Business page ...' }, ...]`) and :73-82 hardcodes the categories (`const categories = [{ name: 'Getting Started', ... }, ...]`), rendered raw at :148 `{cat.name}`, :189 `{faq.question}` and :199 `{faq.answer}`; the search at :92-96 compares against those English strings. The translated content is already shipped and unused — frontend/public/locales/en/help.json:24-55 defines `"categories": { "getting_started": "Getting Started", ... }` and `"faq": { "create_business_q": ..., "create_business_a": ... }`, and hi/help.json returns 
- Fix: Replace the literals with key references, e.g. `{ categoryKey: 'getting_started', qKey: 'faq.create_business_q', aKey: 'faq.create_business_a' }` and render `t(`help:${qKey}`)`, so both display and the search filter operate on the active language.

**Payment-reminder endpoint reports success even when no reminder was sent**
- `packages/notification-service/src/controllers/notification.controller.ts:33` — *false-success*
- Impact: "Reminder sent to <party>" is shown to the user when nothing happened: the party has no phone, the balance is not receivable, or WhatsApp credentials are unset in the environment. Owners believe customers were chased when they were not.
- Evidence: notification.service.ts:166-169 bails out silently — `if (!party || !party.phone) return null; const balance = Number(party.balance); if (balance <= 0) return null;` — and :102-105 also returns null when unconfigured (`if (!WHATSAPP_TOKEN || !WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID) { logger.warn('WhatsApp not configured, skipping message'); return null; }`), while send failures are swallowed at :125-128 (`catch (error) { logger.error('WhatsApp send failed', ...); return null; }`). The controller never inspects the result: notification.controller.ts:32-33 `const result = await notificationService.sendPaymentReminder(businessId, partyId); res.json({ success: true, message: 'Payment reminder sent', data: result });
- Fix: Return `{ sent: false, reason: 'NO_PHONE' | 'NOT_RECEIVABLE' | 'WHATSAPP_UNCONFIGURED' | 'SEND_FAILED' }` from the service, respond 422/200 accordingly, and have both call sites branch on `data.sent` — including replacing PartyLedgerPage's success-in-catch with toast.error.

**Three implemented endpoints are unreachable: user bank-accounts (POST/GET/DELETE) and DELETE /notifications/:id**
- `packages/profile-service/src/routes/profile.routes.ts:31` — *dead-endpoint*
- Impact: The user-level bank-account feature (used for payouts/UPI) cannot be exercised from web or mobile — there is no client method and no UI — and notifications can never be deleted, so a user's feed only ever grows. Anything that does reach addBankAccount unvalidated 500s instead of returning a field error.
- Evidence: Backend exists: profile.routes.ts:31-33 `router.post('/bank-accounts', ctrl.addBankAccount); router.get('/bank-accounts', ctrl.listBankAccounts); router.delete('/bank-accounts/:accountId', ctrl.deleteBankAccount);` and notification.routes.ts:17 `router.delete('/:notificationId', ctrl.deleteNotification);`. Client has neither: api.ts:417-445 profileApi lists only get/update/uploadAvatar/removeAvatar/parties/cutters/expense-types (the only bank-account methods in the file are business-scoped — api.ts:287-290 `listBankAccounts: (bizId) => api.get(`/business/${bizId}/bank-accounts`)`), and api.ts:448-453 notificationApi is `{ list, markRead, markAllRead, sendReminder }` with no delete; Notificat
- Fix: Either add `bankAccounts`/`addBankAccount`/`deleteBankAccount` to profileApi plus a bank-accounts card on ProfilePage and a `delete: (id) => api.delete(`/notifications/${id}`)` method with a per-row delete button, or remove the unused routes. Add a zod schema (accountName, accountNumber, ifscCode, bankName required) to the bank-account POST.

**Duplicate email on profile save surfaces as a bare 500 "Internal server error"**
- `packages/profile-service/src/shared/utils/errors.ts:89` — *error-handling*
- Impact: A user entering an email already registered to another account gets "Internal server error" with no hint about which field is wrong, so the save looks broken rather than rejected.
- Evidence: schema.prisma:24 makes it unique — `email String? @unique` — and profile.service.ts:30-37 writes it with no pre-check: `await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: userId }, data: { ...(data.email !== undefined && { email: data.email }) } });`. The resulting Prisma P2002 is not an AppError, so errors.ts:67-93 falls through to `res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'Internal server error' : err.message });`, which ProfilePage.tsx:68 renders as-is: `toast.error(err.response?.data?.message || t('profile:profile_update_error'));`.
- Fix: Catch P2002 in errorHandler (or check for an existing user before update) and return 409 with `{ message: 'This email is already in use', errors: [{ field: 'email' }] }`.

**Four "Resources" links on the Help page go nowhere (href="#")**
- `frontend/src/components/help/HelpSupportPage.tsx:285` — *dead-ui*
- Impact: User Guide, Video Tutorials, Release Notes and Community Forum look like external links (each carries an ExternalLink icon) but clicking them just jumps to the top of the page, so users looking for documentation hit a dead end.
- Evidence: HelpSupportPage.tsx:285-300 — `<a href="#" className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded hover:bg-muted transition-colors text-sm"><span>{t('help:user_guide')}</span><ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" /></a>` repeated for `help:video_tutorials`, `help:release_notes`, `help:community_forum` (labels defined at en/help.json:20-23). No target URLs exist anywhere in the codebase.
- Fix: Point them at real URLs (docs site / YouTube / CHANGELOG / community) with target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer", or hide the card until the destinations exist.

### Purchases

**Unpaid expenses create a supplier PARTY_PAYABLE ledger entry but never move the party balance**
- `packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:268` — *data-bug*
- Impact: Marking a purchase expense as Unpaid records a credit against the supplier in the ledger, but the supplier's Party.balance is not increased (partyBalanceAmount deliberately excludes expenses). The supplier's balance on the Parties page and the ledger-derived outstanding report therefore disagree by the unpaid expense amount, and the unpaid expense never shows up as money owed on the party card.
- Evidence: Ledger credit is written for the supplier party — packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:268-283: `if (!isPaid) { await tx.ledgerEntry.create({ data: { ... party_id: data.partyId, account_type: 'PARTY_PAYABLE', entry_type: 'CREDIT', amount: new Prisma.Decimal(expense.amount), narration: \`Unpaid expense (${expense.expenseCategory}) for purchase ${purchaseNumber}\` ... } }); }` But the balance update excludes expenses by construction — purchase.service.ts:121 `const partyAmount = subtotal + gstAmount - discount + roundOff;` / :130 `const partyBalanceAmount = Math.max(0, partyAmount - paidAmount);` / :435-441 `await tx.party.update({ where: { id: data.partyId }, data: { bal
- Fix: Decide the owner of an unpaid expense: either increment the supplier's Party.balance by the unpaid expense total alongside the ledger entry, or point the payable entry at the actual expense payee/vendor party instead of the supplier.

**Soft-deleting a purchase leaves its Payment rows behind**
- `packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:671` — *data-bug*
- Impact: After deleting a purchase from the detail page, the payments recorded against it remain in the payments table with reference_type PURCHASE, so they keep appearing in Billing/payment lists and cash totals for a purchase that no longer exists, while its ledger entries were removed — cash reports and the ledger disagree.
- Evidence: deletePurchase removes cutter rows and ledger entries but not payments — packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:670-674: `// Remove cutter transactions tied to this purchase (cascade-sync)\n await tx.cutterTransaction.deleteMany({ where: { purchase_id: purchaseId } });\n // Remove ledger entries created by this purchase ...\n await tx.ledgerEntry.deleteMany({ where: { purchase_id: purchaseId } });` then :688-691 `return tx.purchase.update({ where: { id: purchaseId }, data: { status: 'CANCELLED' } });` — no `tx.payment.deleteMany`, even though updatePurchase does exactly that at :835 `await tx.payment.deleteMany({ where: { reference_id: purchaseId, reference_type: 'PURCHAS
- Fix: Delete (or mark reversed) the purchase's Payment rows and its PaymentReminder rows inside deletePurchase, matching updatePurchase.

**Cancelled purchases stay viewable and their Edit button fails with a generic 500**
- `packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:505` — *broken-handler*
- Impact: A deleted purchase is gone from the list but its detail URL still renders normally (browser back after delete, or a bookmarked link). Clicking Edit on it opens a fully prefilled form; pressing Update Purchase fails with the generic "Failed to save purchase" toast and a 500 in the logs, with no indication that the purchase was deleted.
- Evidence: Read ignores status — packages/purchase-service/src/services/purchase.service.ts:505-506: `const purchase = await prisma.purchase.findFirst({ where: { id: purchaseId, business_id: businessId }, ...` — while the list filters it out (:558-561 `const where: any = { business_id: businessId, status: 'ACTIVE' };`) and the writes require ACTIVE (:794-798 `const existing = await tx.purchase.findFirst({ where: { id: purchaseId, business_id: businessId, status: 'ACTIVE' }, ... }); if (!existing) throw new Error('Purchase not found');`). A bare Error becomes a 500 — packages/purchase-service/src/shared/utils/errors.ts:89-92: `res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'pro
- Fix: Filter getPurchaseById by `status: { not: 'CANCELLED' }` (or return the status and have the page render a "deleted" state with Edit disabled), and throw `new NotFoundError('Purchase not found')`/AppError(404) instead of a bare Error in updatePurchase/deletePurchase.

### Sales

**'Export PDF' in the Export dropdown does nothing but shows a success toast**
- `frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:75` — *dead-ui*
- Impact: On both /sales and /sales/:id the user opens the Export dropdown and picks 'Export PDF'. A green success toast appears and no file is produced — the user waits for a download that never arrives. (CSV, Excel and JSON in the same menu do work, client-side.)
- Evidence: frontend/src/components/shared/ExportButton.tsx:63-77: `if (data && data.length > 0) { ... if (format === 'csv' || format === 'excel') { const csv = dataToCSV(data, columns); downloadFile(csv, `${filename}_${timestamp}.csv`, 'text/csv'); ... } else if (format === 'json') { ... } else { toast.success(t('pdf_coming_soon')); } }` — the pdf branch only toasts, and it uses toast.success so it reads as a completed export. The menu item is unconditionally offered at :115-118: `<DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => handleExport('pdf')}><FileText className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />{t('export_pdf')}</DropdownMenuItem>`. Used by both sales pages: frontend/src/components/sales/SalesPage.tsx:154-158 and frontend/src/
- Fix: Hide the PDF item unless an `onExport` handler is supplied, or change the toast to `toast('PDF export is not available yet')` (non-success) so it does not read as a completed download.

### Subscription & Referrals

**Subscribing with no business yet returns a raw 500 ("No business found for user") on the /subscription?setup=true page**
- `packages/subscription-service/src/services/subscription.service.ts:72` — *error-handling*
- Impact: /subscription is reachable with zero businesses (allowNoBusiness), so a brand-new user who lands there and picks a plan gets a generic "Internal server error" toast in production with no hint that they must create a business first.
- Evidence: A plain Error is thrown, not an AppError: `if (!primaryBusinessUser) throw new Error('No business found for user');` (subscription.service.ts:72) — same for `if (!plan) throw new Error('Plan not found');` (subscription.service.ts:63) — and the handler maps non-AppError to 500 with a masked message: `res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'Internal server error' : err.message })` (packages/subscription-service/src/shared/utils/errors.ts:89-92). The page is explicitly reachable without a business: `<Route path="/subscription" element={<ProtectedRoute allowWhenExpired allowNoBusiness><SubscriptionPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />` (frontend/src/App.ts
- Fix: Throw `new BadRequestError('Create a business before choosing a plan')` / `new NotFoundError('Plan')` so the real 400/404 message reaches the toast, and hide/disable the plan buttons (or route to business creation) when `businesses.length === 0`.

### WhatsApp AI service

**All 5 ESLint errors confirmed; none of them changes runtime behaviour**
- `packages/whatsapp-ai-service/src/mcp/tools.ts:406` — *lint*
- Impact: No user-visible impact. `npm run lint` fails, which blocks CI and hides the real defects reported above behind noise. Recording the per-error runtime verdict as requested.
- Evidence: Ran `node_modules/.bin/eslint packages/whatsapp-ai-service/src/` → `✖ 15 problems (5 errors, 10 warnings)`, exactly the five named: (1) `jaanch.agent.ts 309:11 error 'product' is never reassigned. Use 'const' instead prefer-const` — source jaanch.agent.ts:309 `let product = charIndex * ((i % 2 === 0) ? 1 : 2);`, assigned once inside the loop body, NO runtime change (I hand-verified the checksum against 27AAPFU0939F1ZV: computed check char 'V' == gstin[14] 'V', so the algorithm is correct). (2) `samajh.agent.ts 330:13 error 'value' is never reassigned prefer-const` — source samajh.agent.ts:330 `let value = match[1].replace(/,/g, '');`, NO runtime change. (3)+(4) `samajh.agent.ts 365:18` and `
- Fix: `node_modules/.bin/eslint packages/whatsapp-ai-service/src/ --fix` clears the two prefer-const errors; change `[\/\-.]` to `[/\-.]` on samajh.agent.ts:365; wrap the mcp/tools.ts default clause in braces — or, preferably, delete mcp/tools.ts per the duplicate-registry finding, which removes that error entirely.
